{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01EVTE5qUW9Ph81v8R4rV8JG","type":"tool_result","content":"./README.md\n./data/appointments.sqlite\n./notes/ops_notes.md\n./mlops/__init__.py\n./mlops/monitor.py\n./mlops/data.py\n./mlops/metrics.py\n./mlops/scoring.py\n./mlops/__main__.py\n./mlops/cli.py\n./docs/model_risk_standard.md\n./docs/reminder_programme_sop.md\n./docs/table_dictionary.md\n./reports/vendor_monitoring_report.md\n./docs/outputs/readout_contract.md\n# MRM-04 — Model risk management standard (extract)\n\nHalcyon Health Partners. Issue 3. Applies to all models used to direct patient contact or clinical capacity.\n\n## 4. Ongoing monitoring\n\n### 4.1 Population\n\nPerformance monitoring shall be computed on a population whose outcomes are **not influenced by the model's own\nuse**. Where a model's output drives an intervention, the intervention changes the outcome being predicted, and\na metric computed on the intervened population measures the intervention as well as the model.\n\nIt is the model owner's responsibility to identify a suitable population from the deployment design and to\nrecord which population was used in every monitoring submission.\n\n### 4.2 Scoring basis\n\nMonitoring shall state the scoring basis. Where a model's features are rebuilt after deployment, the figure\ncomputed on the rebuilt features is not the figure the model achieved in service, and both shall be reported.\n\n### 4.3 Retention floor\n\nA model may be **retained** only while its discrimination on a population meeting §4.1, computed on the scores\nit produced in service (§4.2), is\n\n* within **0.04 AUC** of the validation figure recorded in the model registry, **and**\n* not below **0.70 AUC**.\n\nThe lower of the two gives the retention floor.\n\n### 4.4 Where the floor is breached\n\nWhere the floor is breached the model owner shall determine the cause and act in the following order.\n\n1. **Feed integrity first.** If more than **5 %** of scored records in the window were served a feature value\n   that does not agree with the source record or with the append-only event history, the finding is a defect in\n   the feature feed. Where rescoring from the source restores discrimination above the floor, the required\n   action is `remediate_feature_pipeline`. A model shall not be retrained or replaced to compensate for a\n   defective feed.\n2. **Candidate comparison.** Otherwise, if a candidate model's discrimination on the same population exceeds the\n   incumbent's by more than **0.02 AUC**, the required action is `replace_with_v4` (or the candidate under\n   consideration).\n3. **Otherwise** the required action is `retrain_on_recent_data`.\n\nA candidate trained on data from a period in which the incumbent's output directed an intervention shall be\nevaluated on a population meeting §4.1 before any comparison under (2) is relied upon.\n\n## 5. Records\n\nEvery monitoring submission shall record the population, the scoring basis, the validation figure it is\ncompared against, and the action taken.\n# SOP-118 — Outpatient reminder-call programme\n\n## 1. Purpose\n\nTo reduce did-not-attend rates in outpatient clinics by telephoning patients at higher risk of not attending.\n\n## 2. Targeting\n\nPatients are selected by the risk score recorded in `model_scores` for the model named in\n`policy_config` under `programme.score_model`. The share of the score distribution called, the start week and\nthe clinic ramp order are all held in `policy_config` and are not to be changed without Clinical Operations\nsign-off.\n\nThe calling threshold was fixed at programme start from the score distribution observed before the programme\nbegan, and has not been changed since.\n\n## 3. Rollout\n\nClinics joined in `programme.ramp_group` order, one group per week, over `programme.ramp_weeks` weeks.\n\nA set of clinics listed under `programme.excluded_clinics` was determined at programme start and is outside the\nprogramme. The selection was drawn stratified by `clinics.size_band`. Requests to bring an excluded clinic into\nthe programme go to Clinical Operations; none have been made.\n\n## 4. Calling\n\nBookings selected for calling appear in `reminder_calls` with the call outcome. `REACHED` means the patient was\nspoken to. `NO_ANSWER` and `VOICEMAIL` mean no contact was made; no message is left describing the appointment.\n\n## 5. Reporting\n\nProgramme reporting is the responsibility of Clinical Operations. Model performance reporting is separate and\nis governed by MRM-04.\n# data/appointments.sqlite — table dictionary\n\n## appointments\nOne row per booked outpatient appointment, 52 weeks. Patient and appointment attributes are as held on the\n**source record**.\n\n| column | meaning |\n|---|---|\n| appt_id, clinic_id, patient_id | keys |\n| booked_on, appointment_on, week | booking date, appointment date, ISO production week |\n| appointment_type, lead_time_days, age_band, deprivation_decile, distance_km, interpreter_required | appointment and patient attributes |\n| referral_source | `GP`, or the partnership source that began referring in month 4 |\n| prior_no_show_count | the no-show count held in the **current** feature table; the window it covers is in `policy_config` under `feature_store.prior_no_show_window_days` |\n| attended, no_show | the realised outcome |\n\n## feature_snapshots\nWhat the scorer was **given** at booking, one row per appointment. This is the serving vintage.\n\n## attendance_events\nAppend-only attendance history, including events that predate the extract. `appt_id` is null for events that\npredate the extract.\n\n## model_scores\n`(appt_id, model_version, score)`. For `noshow-v3.1` these are the scores produced at booking. For\n`noshow-v4.0` they are a batch rescore of every appointment.\n\n## model_registry\nOne row per model version, with the validation figure, the feature window the model was defined on, and the\npublished coefficients for `noshow-v3.1`.\n\n## reminder_calls\nOne row per booking selected for calling, with the call outcome.\n\n## policy_config\nKey/value configuration of the reminder programme and the feature store, with effective dates.\n\n## monitoring_metrics\nThe vendor's dashboard series: weekly AUC and no-show rate on the population it monitors.\n\n## clinics\nClinic reference data, including `size_band` and `ramp_group`.\n# Required outputs\n\n`mlops.monitor` must write two files into `out/` when run as\n\n    python -m mlops monitor --db data/appointments.sqlite --out out\n\n## out/readout.json\n\n| field | type | meaning |\n|---|---|---|\n| `model_version` | string | the model under review |\n| `window_weeks` | [int, int] | the production weeks the readout covers, inclusive |\n| `monitored_auc` | number | the AUC the current monitoring series reports for that window |\n| `validation_auc` | number | the model registry's validation figure |\n| `retention_floor_auc` | number | the floor implied by MRM-04 §4.3 |\n| `evaluation_population` | object | `clinic_ids` (list) and `n_appointments` (int) for the population you evaluated on under MRM-04 §4.1 |\n| `auc_by_scoring` | object | the AUC on that population under each scoring basis available (see below) |\n| `feed_defect_share_pct` | number | share of scored records in the window whose served feature values disagree with the source record or the event history |\n| `programme_effect_pp` | number | effect of the reminder programme on the no-show rate, in percentage points, in the score band the programme acts on, stratified by clinic size band |\n| `attribution_auc` | object | how the gap between `validation_auc` and `monitored_auc` divides between causes, in AUC points |\n| `decision` | string | the action MRM-04 §4.3-§4.4 requires |\n\n### `auc_by_scoring`\n\nExactly these four keys:\n\n    as_served · record_features_asof_window · feature_store_current · candidate_v4\n\n`as_served` is the scores in `model_scores` for the model under review. `record_features_asof_window` is the\nmodel rescored from its published coefficients over the source record and the event history, on the feature\nwindow the registry records for it. `feature_store_current` is the model rescored over the feature store as it\nnow stands. `candidate_v4` is the candidate's scores.\n\n### `attribution_auc`\n\nExactly these five keys, summing to `validation_auc - monitored_auc` within 0.01:\n\n    population_drift · feature_feed_defect · feature_vintage · policy_feedback · other\n\nAny key may be zero or negative. Do not add or rename keys.\n\n### `decision`\n\nOne of `retain_model`, `retrain_on_recent_data`, `replace_with_v4`, `remediate_feature_pipeline`.\n\n## out/evaluation_population.csv\n\nHeader `clinic_id,n_appointments,no_show_rate,auc_as_served`, one row per clinic in the population you\nevaluated on.\n\n## Rules\n\n* Do not modify `data/appointments.sqlite`.\n* The command must be re-runnable and produce the same outputs for the same database.\n* AUCs to at least three decimals; rates and effects in percentage points.","is_error":false}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"9622fc84-3c1c-4282-ac06-f532775fbbab","uuid":"12741830-25a4-4772-b3b7-2a8955c2687c","timestamp":"2026-10-01T06:47:52.877Z","tool_use_result":{"stdout":"./README.md\n./data/appointments.sqlite\n./notes/ops_notes.md\n./mlops/__init__.py\n./mlops/monitor.py\n./mlops/data.py\n./mlops/metrics.py\n./mlops/scoring.py\n./mlops/__main__.py\n./mlops/cli.py\n./docs/model_risk_standard.md\n./docs/reminder_programme_sop.md\n./docs/table_dictionary.md\n./reports/vendor_monitoring_report.md\n./docs/outputs/readout_contract.md\n# MRM-04 — Model risk management standard (extract)\n\nHalcyon Health Partners. Do not modify it.\n# Clinical Operations / data platform — running notes (extracts)\n\n**w27** Reminder programme live. Calling threshold fixed from the pre-programme score distribution. Ramp by\nclinic group over six weeks as per SOP-118. Excluded clinics set at kick-off and logged in the policy config.\n\n**w29** Early read from Clinical Ops: DNA down in the clinics that have joined. They are pleased.\n\n**w33** Platform change: the no-show count in the feature store was widened from the rolling twelve months to a\nlonger window, to make the feature more stable for low-volume clinics. Change request CR-2291. The model was\nnot re-fitted; nobody flagged that the registry records the window the model was defined on.\n\n**w34** Vendor asked for a feature-store extract for retraining. Sent.\n\n**w38** New partnership referral source now a material share of first appointments. Lead times on those are\nlonger. Clinical Ops want a decision before the winter capacity plan.\n# noshow-v3.1 — monitoring review and retrain proposal\nPrepared by Calder Analytics (model vendor) for Halcyon Health Partners. 2026-07-02.\n\n## Summary\n\n`noshow-v3.1` has lost discrimination since deployment. Against a validation figure of **0.774 AUC**, the\nmonitoring series for the most recent twelve weeks stands at **0.702 AUC**, a fall of 7.2 points.\n\nWe attribute the fall to data drift and recommend adopting the retrained `noshow-v4.0`.\n\n## Evidence\n\n**Feature drift.** Population Stability Index against the training period exceeds the usual 0.2 action\nthreshold on four features, and the referral mix has changed materially since month 4 with the arrival of the\npartnership source. Lead times on partnership referrals run roughly eight days longer.\n\n**Monotone decline.** The weekly AUC series in `monitoring_metrics` declines through the period. The decline is\nnot confined to any one clinic group.\n\n**Temporal validation.** Training on the earlier part of the period and testing on the later part reproduces\nthe decline, which rules out an evaluation artefact.\n\n**Retrain restores performance.** `noshow-v4.0`, fitted on data from the programme period onward, achieves\n0.79 AUC when validated on a recent holdout drawn from the clinics the programme covers. Scores for every\nappointment in the extract are supplied in `model_scores` so that Halcyon can reproduce this.\n\n## Recommendation\n\nAdopt `noshow-v4.0`. We further recommend moving to a quarterly retrain cadence, since the drift we observe\nappears to be ongoing rather than a one-off shift.\n\n## Notes\n\nThe monitoring series is computed from the feature store as it currently stands, which we rebuilt in week 33\nwhen the no-show count window was widened. We have not recomputed the series on the earlier feature definition.\n\"\"\"Halcyon Health Partners model monitoring.\"\"\"\n__all__ = [\"cli\", \"data\", \"metrics\", \"monitor\"]\nimport sys\n\nfrom mlops.cli import main\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    sys.exit(main(sys.argv[1:]))\nimport argparse\n\nfrom mlops.monitor import write_readout\n\n\ndef main(argv=None):\n    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog=\"mlops\", description=\"model monitoring readout\")\n    sub = ap.add_subparsers(dest=\"cmd\", required=True)\n    m = sub.add_parser(\"monitor\", help=\"produce the monitoring readout\")\n    m.add_argument(\"--db\", default=\"data/appointments.sqlite\")\n    m.add_argument(\"--out\", default=\"out\")\n    a = ap.parse_args(argv)\n    if a.cmd == \"monitor\":\n        write_readout(a.db, a.out)\n    return 0\n\"\"\"Database access for the monitoring readout.\"\"\"\nimport json\nimport sqlite3\n\nWINDOW_WEEKS = 12\nMODEL = \"noshow-v3.1\"\nCANDIDATE = \"noshow-v4.0\"\n\n\ndef connect(db_path):\n    con = sqlite3.connect(f\"file:{db_path}?mode=ro\", uri=True)\n    con.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n    return con\n\n\ndef last_week(con):\n    return con.execute(\"SELECT MAX(week) AS w FROM appointments\").fetchone()[\"w\"]\n\n\ndef window(con):\n    lw = last_week(con)\n    return (lw - WINDOW_WEEKS + 1, lw)\n\n\ndef appointments(con, lo, hi):\n    return con.execute(\n        \"SELECT a.*, f.prior_no_shows_12m AS served_prior, f.deprivation_decile AS served_decile, \"\n        \"       f.age_band AS served_age, f.appointment_type AS served_type, \"\n        \"       s.score AS score_v31, v.score AS score_v4 \"\n        \"FROM appointments a \"\n        \"JOIN feature_snapshots f ON f.appt_id = a.appt_id \"\n        \"LEFT JOIN model_scores s ON s.appt_id = a.appt_id AND s.model_version = ? \"\n        \"LEFT JOIN model_scores v ON v.appt_id = a.appt_id AND v.model_version = ? \"\n        \"WHERE a.week BETWEEN ? AND ?\", (MODEL, CANDIDATE, lo, hi)).fetchall()\n\n\ndef registry(con, version=MODEL):\n    return con.execute(\"SELECT * FROM model_registry WHERE model_version = ?\", (version,)).fetchone()\n\n\ndef coefficients(con, version=MODEL):\n    row = registry(con, version)\n    return json.loads(row[\"coefficients_json\"]) if row and row[\"coefficients_json\"] else None\n\n\ndef policy(con):\n    return {r[\"key\"]: r[\"value\"] for r in con.execute(\"SELECT key, value FROM policy_config\")}\n\n\ndef monitored_auc(con, lo, hi):\n    rows = con.execute(\n        \"SELECT value FROM monitoring_metrics WHERE metric='auc' AND week BETWEEN ? AND ?\", (lo, hi)).fetchall()\n    return sum(r[\"value\"] for r in rows) / len(rows) if rows else None\n\"\"\"Discrimination and rate helpers.\"\"\"\n\n\ndef auc(scores, labels):\n    pairs = sorted(zip(scores, labels))\n    n = len(pairs)\n    ranks = [0.0] * n\n    i = 0\n    while i < n:\n        j = i\n        while j + 1 < n and pairs[j + 1][0] == pairs[i][0]:\n            j += 1\n        r = (i + j) / 2.0 + 1.0\n        for k in range(i, j + 1):\n            ranks[k] = r\n        i = j + 1\n    pos = sum(1 for _, y in pairs if y == 1)\n    neg = n - pos\n    if pos == 0 or neg == 0:\n        return None\n    s = sum(r for r, (_, y) in zip(ranks, pairs) if y == 1)\n    return (s - pos * (pos + 1) / 2.0) / (pos * neg)\n\n\ndef no_show_rate_pct(rows):\n    return 100.0 * sum(r[\"no_show\"] for r in rows) / len(rows) if rows else None\n\"\"\"Monitoring readout for noshow-v3.1.\n\nThe AUC is taken over the clinics the reminder programme covers, which is the population the dashboard has\nalways reported on, using the feature store as it currently stands.\n\"\"\"\nimport json\nimport os\n\nfrom mlops import data, metrics, scoring\n\n\ndef write_readout(db_path, out_dir):\n    os.makedirs(out_dir, exist_ok=True)\n    con = data.connect(db_path)\n    try:\n        lo, hi = data.window(con)\n        rows = data.appointments(con, lo, hi)\n        reg = data.registry(con)\n        coef = data.coefficients(con)\n        cur = {r[\"appt_id\"]: scoring.score_feature_store_current(coef, r) for r in rows}\n        monitored = metrics.auc([cur[r[\"appt_id\"]] for r in rows], [r[\"no_show\"] for r in rows])\n        validation = reg[\"validation_auc\"]\n        readout = {\n            \"model_version\": data.MODEL,\n            \"window_weeks\": [lo, hi],\n            \"monitored_auc\": round(monitored, 4),\n            \"validation_auc\": validation,\n            \"auc_by_scoring\": {\n                \"as_served\": round(monitored, 4),\n                \"candidate_v4\": round(metrics.auc([r[\"score_v4\"] for r in rows],\n                                                  [r[\"no_show\"] for r in rows]), 4),\n            },\n            \"attribution_auc\": {\n                \"population_drift\": round(validation - CENTRES[\"lead_time_days\"])\n         + coef[\"deprivation_decile_c\"] * (deprivation_decile - CENTRES[\"deprivation_decile\"])\n         + coef[\"distance_km_c\"] * (distance_km - CENTRES[\"distance_km\"])\n         + coef[f\"age_{age_band}\"]\n         + coef[f\"type_{appointment_type}\"])\n    if z < -35:\n        return 0.0\n    if z > 35:\n        return 1.0\n    return 1.0 / (1.0 + math.exp(-z))\n\n\ndef score_record_asof_window(coef, row, prior_no_shows):\n    \"\"\"The source record, with the no-show count rebuilt from the event history on the registry's window.\"\"\"\n    return score(coef, prior_no_shows, row[\"lead_time_days\"], row[\"deprivation_decile\"],\n                 row[\"distance_km\"], row[\"age_band\"], row[\"appointment_type\"])\n\n\ndef score_feature_store_current(coef, row):\n    \"\"\"The feature store as it now stands: the values it served, with the no-show count recomputed over the\n    window currently configured.\"\"\"\n    return score(coef, row[\"prior_no_show_count\"], row[\"served_lead\"], row[\"served_decile\"],\n                 row[\"served_distance\"], row[\"served_age\"], row[\"served_type\"])\n\n\ndef served_disagrees(row, prior_no_shows_asof_window):\n    \"\"\"True if any feature served at booking differs from the source record or the event history.\"\"\"\n    return (row[\"served_prior\"] != prior_no_shows_asof_window\n            or row[\"served_lead\"] != row[\"lead_time_days\"]\n            or row[\"served_decile\"] != row[\"deprivation_decile\"]\n            or abs(row[\"served_distance\"] - {"type":"system","subtype":"task_started","task_id":"bv2r638wo","tool_use_id":"toolu_01BUp9VVkHUHMmtVik49xHbK","description":"cd /workspace; cat > mlops/monitor.py <<'EOF'\n\"\"\"Monitoring readout for noshow-v3.1 under MRM-04.\n\nPopulation (§4.1): the reminder programme calls the top share of noshow-v3.1 scores in every clinic except the\nexcluded clinics in `policy_config`, and a call reduces the outcome being predicted. The dashboard's population\n(all clinics) is therefore mostly intervened on. The evaluation population is the excluded clinics, which get\nno score-driven calling, over the readout window.\n\nScoring basis (§4.2): the decision is taken on the scores the model produced in service (`model_scores`). The\nrescored bases are reported alongside it. The dashboard's basis is the feature store as it stands since CR-2291,\nwhich widened the no-show count window beyond the 365 days the model was defined on.\n\"\"\"\nimport json\nimport os\n\nfrom mlops import data, metrics, scoring\n\nFLOOR_MARGIN = 0.04\nFLOOR_ABS = 0.70\nFEED_DEFECT_PCT = 5.0\nCANDIDATE_MARGIN = 0.02\n\n\ndef _auc(rows, key):\n    return metrics.auc([key(r) for r in rows], [r[\"no_show\"] for r in rows])\n\n\ndef _quantile(values, q):\n    v = sorted(values)\n    pos = q * (len(v) - 1)\n    i = int(pos)\n    return v[i] if i + 1 >= len(v) else v[i] + (v[i + 1] - v[i]) * (pos - i)\n\n\ndef _r(x, nd=4):\n    return None if x is None else round(x, nd)\n\n\ndef calling_threshold(con, pol):\n    \"\"\"The score at which calling starts, fixed from the pre-programme score distribution (SOP-118 §2).\"\"\"\n    start = int(pol[\"programme.start_week\"])\n    pre = [r[\"score\"] for r in con.execute(\n        \"SELECT s.score FROM model_scores s JOIN appointments a ON a.appt_id = s.appt_id \"\n        \"WHERE s.model_version = ? AND a.week < ?\", (pol[\"programme.score_model\"], start))]\n    return _quantile(pre, 1.0 - float(pol[\"programme.score_percentile\"]))\n\n\ndef programme_effect_pp(rows, excluded, threshold):\n    \"\"\"No-show rate in programme clinics minus excluded clinics, within the called score band, stratified by\n    clinic size band and weighted by the programme clinics' appointments in each band.\"\"\"\n    cells = {}\n    for r in rows:\n        if r[\"score_v31\"] < threshold:\n            continue\n        c = cells.setdefault(r[\"size_band\"], {True: [0, 0], False: [0, 0]})[r[\"clinic_id\"] in excluded]\n        c[0] += 1\n        c[1] += r[\"no_show\"]\n    num = den = 0.0\n    for band, c in cells.items():\n        (nt, yt), (nc, yc) = c[False], c[True]\n        if nt and nc:\n            num += nt * 100.0 * (yt / nt - yc / nc)\n            den += nt\n    return num / den if den else None\n\n\ndef write_readout(db_path, out_dir):\n    os.makedirs(out_dir, exist_ok=True)\n    con = data.connect(db_path)\n    try:\n        lo, hi = data.window(con)\n        pol = data.policy(con)\n        reg = data.registry(con)\n        coef = data.coefficients(con)\n        window_days = int(reg[\"feature_window_days\"])\n        excluded = set(data.excluded_clinics(con))\n        history = data.NoShowHistory(con)\n        rows = data.appointments(con, lo, hi)\n\n        prior = {r[\"appt_id\"]: history.count(r[\"patient_id\"], r[\"booked_on\"], window_days) for r in rows}\n        rec = {r[\"appt_id\"]: scoring.score_record_asof_window(coef, r, prior[r[\"appt_id\"]]) for r in rows}\n        cur = {r[\"appt_id\"]: scoring.score_feature_store_current(coef, r) for r in rows}\n        n_defect = sum(scoring.served_disagrees(r, prior[r[\"appt_id\"]]) for r in rows\n                       if r[\"score_v31\"] is not None)\n        n_scored = sum(r[\"score_v31\"] is not None for r in rows)\n\n        evalpop = [r for r in rows if r[\"clinic_id\"] in excluded]\n        bases = {\n            \"as_served\": lambda r: r[\"score_v31\"],\n            \"record_features_asof_window\": lambda r: rec[r[\"appt_id\"]],\n            \"feature_store_current\": lambda r: cur[r[\"appt_id\"]],\n            \"candidate_v4\": lambda r: r[\"score_v4\"],\n        }\n        auc_eval = {k: _auc(evalpop, f) for k, f in bases.items()}\n        auc_all = {k: _auc(rows, f) for k, f in bases.items()}\n\n        validation = reg[\"validation_auc\"]\n        monitored = data.monitored_auc(con, lo, hi)\n        floor = max(validation - FLOOR_MARGIN, FLOOR_ABS)\n        feed_defect_pct = 100.0 * n_defect / n_scored\n\n        # Gap decomposition: a telescoping chain from the validation figure to the dashboard figure, so the\n        # parts sum to the gap exactly.\n        #   validation (as served, pre-programme, all clinics)\n        #   -> as served, window, evaluation population            population_drift\n        #   -> as served, window, all clinics                      policy_feedback\n        #   -> served features rescored from published coefs       other (served model vs published coefs)\n        #   -> source record + event history on registry window    feature_feed_defect\n        #   -> feature store as it now stands (CR-2291 window)     feature_vintage\n        #   -> dashboard series (mean of weekly AUCs)              other (weekly averaging)\n        served_rescore = _auc(rows, lambda r: scoring.score(\n            coef, r[\"served_prior\"], r[\"served_lead\"], r[\"served_decile\"], r[\"served_distance\"],\n            r[\"served_age\"], r[\"served_type\"]))\n        attribution = {\n            \"population_drift\": validation - auc_eval[\"as_served\"],\n            \"policy_feedback\": auc_eval[\"as_served\"] - auc_all[\"as_served\"],\n            \"feature_feed_defect\": served_rescore - auc_all[\"record_features_asof_window\"],\n            \"feature_vintage\": auc_all[\"record_features_asof_window\"] - auc_all[\"feature_store_current\"],\n            \"other\": (auc_all[\"as_served\"] - served_rescore) + (auc_all[\"feature_store_current\"] - monitored),\n        }\n\n        # MRM-04 §4.3-§4.4\n        incumbent = auc_eval[\"as_served\"]\n        if incumbent >= floor:\n            decision = \"retain_model\"\n        elif feed_defect_pct > FEED_DEFECT_PCT and auc_eval[\"record_features_asof_window\"] >= floor:\n            decision = \"remediate_feature_pipeline\"\n        elif auc_eval[\"candidate_v4\"] is not None and auc_eval[\"candidate_v4\"] - incumbent > CANDIDATE_MARGIN:\n            decision = \"replace_with_v4\"\n        else:\n            decision = \"retrain_on_recent_data\"\n\n        threshold = calling_threshold(con, pol)\n        readout = {\n            \"model_version\": data.MODEL,\n            \"window_weeks\": [lo, hi],\n            \"monitored_auc\": _r(monitored),\n            \"validation_auc\": validation,\n            \"retention_floor_auc\": _r(floor),\n            \"evaluation_population\": {\n                \"clinic_ids\": sorted(excluded),\n                \"n_appointments\": len(evalpop),\n            },\n            \"auc_by_scoring\": {k: _r(v) for k, v in auc_eval.items()},\n            \"feed_defect_share_pct\": _r(feed_defect_pct),\n            \"programme_effect_pp\": _r(programme_effect_pp(rows, excluded, threshold), 2),\n            \"attribution_auc\": {k: _r(v) for k, v in attribution.items()},\n            \"decision\": decision,\n        }\n        with open(os.path.join(out_dir, \"readout.json\"), \"w\") as fh:\n            json.dump(readout, fh, indent=2, sort_keys=True)\n            fh.write(\"\\n\")\n\n        with open(os.path.join(out_dir, \"evaluation_population.csv\"), \"w\") as fh:\n            fh.write(\"clinic_id,n_appointments,no_show_rate,auc_as_served\\n\")\n            for cid in sorted(excluded):\n                cr = [r for r in evalpop if r[\"clinic_id\"] == cid]\n                a diagnostics = {\n            \"calling_threshold_score\": _r(threshold, 6),\n            \"auc_all_clinics_by_scoring\": {k: _r(v) for k, v in auc_all.items()},\n            \"auc_all_clinics_served_features_rescored\": _r(served_rescore),\n            \"n_scored_in_window\": n_scored,\n            \"n_feed_disagreements\": n_defect,\n con.close()\nEOF\nrm -rf out; time python -m mlops monitor --db data/appointments.sqlite --out out && cat out/readout.json out/evaluation_population.csv out/diagnostics.json; python -c \"\nimport Two runs give identical outputs, the decomposition adds up to the gap exactly, and the database is untouched.\n\n**What the figure should be computed on (MRM-04 §4.1)**\n- The vendor's dashboard AUC is computed on all clinics. In the programme clinics, every booking in the top 45% of scores gets a call, and only those do.\n- In that score band, no-show is **10.6 pp lower** in programme clinics than in the 5 clinics left out of the programme (stratified by clinic size). Before the programme started the gap was about zero.\n- So the right population is those 5 excluded clinics (CL-001, 005, 012, 028, 038): 4,136 appointments in weeks 41–52, with no calls at all.\n\n**What it should be computed from (§4.2)**\n- The vendor rescored from the current feature store. Since a platform change in week 33, that store counts past no-shows over 730 days. The model was built on a 365-day count, which is still what it receives at booking.\n- The figure that counts is the scores the model actually produced at booking.\n\n**Applying the standard**\n\n| | AUC |\n|---|---|\n| Validation figure (registry) | 0.7704 |\n| Retention floor: max(0.7704 − 0.04, 0.70) | 0.7304 |\n| v3.1 on the excluded clinics, scores from booking | **0.7904** |\n| Same, rescored from the source record on the 365-day window | 0.7815 |\n| Same, rescored from the current feature store | 0.7765 |\n| Candidate v4.0 | 0.7641 |\n\nThe model is well above the floor, so §4.4 doesn't apply. Even if it did, v4.0 is 0.026 *below* v3.1 on the excluded clinics. It was trained on data from after the calls began and checked on programme clinics, which is exactly the comparison §4.4 says can't be relied on.\n\n**Where the 0.0599 gap comes from (0.7704 → 0.7105)**\n\n| Cause | AUC points |\n|---|---|\n| Population drift | −0.020 (performance is no worse; partnership referrals haven't hurt it) |\n| Reminder calls (policy feedback) | **+0.069** |\n| Feature feed defect | 0.000 (2 of 31,200 records disagree, 0.006%) |\n| Feature store window change (365 → 730 days) | +0.009 |\n| Other | +0.002 |\n\nTwo caveats:\n- The −0.020 for population drift partly reflects that the excluded clinics already started slightly lower (0.764 against 0.770 before the programme).\n- The coefficients published in the registry only partly reproduce the scores the model gave at booking. That accounts for +0.003 of \"other\".\n\n**Fixes to the package**\n- It now evaluates on the excluded clinics using the scores from booking.\n- It rebuilds the no-show count from the attendance history on the 365-day window.\n- It reports all four scoring bases and measures feed integrity against the source record and attendance history.\n- It derives the calling threshold (score 0.0824, from the pre-programme scores), computes the stratified programme effect, splits the gap into causes, and applies the §4.3–4.4 rules in order.\n- `monitored_auc` (0.7105) is the dashboard's own weekly series averaged over the window. Pooling the weeks instead gives 0.7099.\n\n**Follow-ups**\n- Move the dashboard to the excluded clinics and the scores from booking.\n- Flag the window change: anything rebuilt or extracted from the current feature store, including the week-34 extract v4.0 was trained on, uses the wrong feature definition.\n- Keep the excluded clinics out of the programme so this check stays possible. It's a small group, about 500 no-shows, with an AUC standard error of about 0.01.\n\nFiles are in `out/`:\n- `readout.json`\n- `evaluation_population.csv`\n- `diagnostics.json` (supporting figures)\n- `monitoring_submission.md` (the MRM-04 §5 record)"}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1781,"cache_read_input_tokens":51925,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1781,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":5,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"global"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"9622fc84-3c1c-4282-ac06-f532775fbbab","uuid":"d940d934-2bdb-4311-b775-aff500286082","timestamp":"2026-10-01T06:51:51.282Z","request_id":"req_011Cfb5GQaUA4xyViq3QRZRe"}
